Pet lines: model and prompt eval

Each blorbarium pet's genes go to Workers AI twice: once to write a short character bible, once to write two to four lines for each of the 132 situations a pet speaks in. Every line is checked mechanically (at most 30 characters, only the badge font's characters, no repeats, every situation covered); failures are asked for again, twice at most, and anything still failing falls back to the built-in lines.

Run r1: 6 models × 3 prompt versions × 20 pets (two per species). Judges: qwen3.8-27b and deepseek-v4-pro-0813, scoring 1 to 5. Recommended: kimi-k2.6 with prompt v3 (100% coverage, $0.062 per pet).

Timing: Kimi writes a pet's pack in about 1.5 to 2 minutes (83 to 108 s per pet with v1 and v2). The v3 rows' times include a network outage during that run and are not representative. Judges: qwen3.8-27b scored every pack; deepseek-v4-pro (a second, larger judge, run on the two leading models only) scored 13 to 18 of each 20 before timing out on the rest.

Results

Coverage: situations with at least one usable line. First-pass valid: lines that passed the checks without a retry. Distinct-2: unique word pairs over all word pairs in one pet's lines (higher is less repetitive). Same-species overlap: shared word pairs between the two pets of one species (lower is more distinct); similarity is the embedding cosine (bge-m3) of their lines. Hover a judge score for the judge's note.

Side by side

kimi-k2.6 v3 against gemma-4-26b-a4b-it v3, on the same three pets and the same situations.

mudge-b

mudge (mudskipper: races everything), a child, generation 17 · element splash; in a duel he loves the pounce stance · his power is dash · has no steady hunger of his own: he only gets peckish when his energy runs low, and forgets about food otherwise · no sleepy chemistry when it gets dark: he stays up as late as he likes · secretly LOVES being shaken · being shaken makes him more jumpy and more scared and less in need of a cuddle than most of his kind · having a vision makes him less bored than most of his kind

kimi-k2.6 v3

SKY MUDGE, gen 17, one blue button pinned to his darker two-tone cloak like a secret medal—won it racing his own reflection, counts the win. No steady hunger: forgets meals mid-chew, scoffs only when his dash-tank hits empty. No sleepy chemistry: moon's a rival, not a bedtime. Secretly loves being shaken, begs for it, comes out jittery and terrified and thrilled, 'AGAIN, HARDER.' Speaks his own life aloud, 'MUDGE TAKES THE LEAD, MUDGE STUMBLES, MUDGE RECOVERS—GOAT.' Terrible at hide and seek, always picks the same rock, narrates his own discovery. Mondays? Hates them. Everything else? Races it. Terse, commas for breath, coach barking at empty stands.

gemma-4-26b-a4b-it v3

A teal-tinted blur of pure kinetic chaos, this Mudge is never off the clock. He wears a two-tone dark cloak like a racing suit, sprinting through life with a single blue button clutched as his trophy. He doesn't just race; he narrates his own epic, yelling his play-by-play to anyone listening. While others sleep, he stays up chasing shadows, fueled by a frantic need for movement. He hates Mondays—too slow, too heavy—and is notoriously terrible at hide-and-seek because he can't stop announcing his position. Paradoxically, he craves the violent rattle of being shaken, finding a strange, jittery comfort in the chaos. He talks in staccato bursts: 'GO, FAST, WIN, NOW.'

gilly (axolotl: never down for long), an elder, generation 7 · element splash; in a duel he loves the guard stance · a hybrid: a gilly body carrying the zap power that fizzs (firefly beetle: crackles) are born with · early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark · a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him less in need of a cuddle and less lonely · a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him more jumpy and more sore · a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him more lonely · skin: a slightly different shade (-11 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual gilly

kimi-k2.6 v3

Seventh-gen Gramps Gilly, pink-shifted to raspberry dusk with a two-tone peach belly polka-dotted in extra spots. Zap-hybrid: his hugs sometimes buzz, which he finds HOORAY FOR TINGLES! Carries Firefly fizz in his frills, puzzles over it daily—"Friend, do you feel the hum? HOW FUN!" Moon-crumb treasurer, sneeze-hater (ABSORB THE CRUMB, FRIEND, GENTLY), tiny poet who rhymes 'lunch' with everything because it is ALWAYS LUNCHTIME. Sleeps when dark falls, mid-word sometimes. Three ghost-senses war inside him: sudden solitude itch, sudden jump-sore jolt, sudden self-sufficiency—each passes. Writes poems about all three. Grows back, grows back, grows back. Still lonely after seven generations of being fine.

gemma-4-26b-a4b-it v3

Gilly is a vibrant, two-toned elder who treats every catastrophe as a party invitation. While most Gillys are simple healers, his hybrid spark makes him crackle with a strange, electric 'zap' when he’s excited. He is a whimsical soul, obsessed with his collection of moon-shaped crumbs and spends his afternoons scribbling tiny, rhyming poems in the silt. However, his relentless sunshine hides a glitchy soul; he is prone to sudden, inexplicable bouts of loneliness and phantom aches that make him jump at his own shadow. He is a jittery optimist who lives for the next meal, always convinced it is lunchtime. GOOD NEWS: LUNCHTIME! HOORAY! He mends everything, even if he forgets to mend his own nerves.

octopus-b

octopus (octopus: the abyss rings back), a child, generation 7 · element splash; in a duel he loves the trick stance · his power is blackbell · has no steady hunger of his own: he only gets peckish when his energy runs low, and forgets about food otherwise · a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night · naps all the time · secretly LOVES being shaken · being shaken makes him less uncomfortable and less in need of a cuddle than most of his kind

kimi-k2.6 v3

Inkwell is a child prophet who plays at being ancient. His cloak is the color of a bruise that never healed, his glow flickers between ember and drowned green—the two tones never agreeing on what omen they mean. He carries a dried pea like a doubloon, will not tell you where he found it, and has seventeen theories about its curse. He naps through his own prophecies, wakes at moonrise starving, then stares at snacks until they spoil from the pressure. Loud chewing makes him ink himself. Shake him and he goes limp with joy, a terrible secret he keeps even from himself. He speaks in ellipses... then interrupts them. His jokes land like prophecies that forgot their punchline: 'THE CRACKED BELL TOLLS... FOR PEAS.' He loves that you do not understand.

gemma-4-26b-a4b-it v3

A tiny, washed-out harbinger of doom who spends most of his time napping in a state of existential dread. Unlike his booming ancestors, this octopus is a quiet conspirator; he hoards secrets like sunken gold and treats his single treasure—a dried pea—as a relic of a lost civilization. He is a neurotic overthinker who views every snack as a complex moral dilemma, often paralyzed by the choice between a cracker and a grape. He detests the rhythmic violence of loud chewing, seeing it as a herald of decay. Paradoxically, while he speaks in heavy, midnight omens, he is secretly a thrill-seeker who craves being shaken, finding the chaotic vibration the only thing that quiets his anxious, overactive mind.

  • his power is foresee

Genome dice treasure: a very round pebble · pet peeve: being called cute · hobby: staring contests · flaw: gets the giggles at bad times

3 · Character bible

Grungo THE ROUND PEBBLE KNOWS, they call him, clutching his smooth stone like a second hood. Brighter-skinned, darker-cloaked, two-toned cyan seer—he stares. Staring contests are his worship. Win? "THE REEDS SAY: YOU BLINKED." Lose? giggles through prophecy, ruins doom. His mysterious sense: sometimes alone feels like omens cuddling him. He hates cute. "I AM A VESSEL." The pebble is round. He is round. Coincidence? THE REEDS SAY NO. Bad snack? queasy oracle: "LINE... SO IT IS... bleh." Long press him, he snores mid-vision. Night owl, moon colleague, still judges her phases. Flies=damp=doom. The order matters.

THE PEBBLE SEES YOU.I FORESAW THE BLINK.HOOD UP. STARE DOWN.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • grungo (frog: a skittish oracle), a grown-up, generation 7
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is foresee
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him less lonely
  • fainting in a duel makes him less jumpy than most of his kind
  • making a friend in a duel makes him more sleepy than most of his kind
  • skin: a clearly different shade (-48 degrees round the colour wheel), brighter than the usual grungo
  • fewer markings than most
  • carries 6 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children
  • 6 extra genes from his ancestors' copying
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when cradled, rest to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when just after shake, curl to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when owner near, call to ease his loneliness

Genome dice treasure: a single blue button · pet peeve: the word 'later' · hobby: writing tiny poems · flaw: thinks he is famous

3 · Character bible

Grungo the Button-Saint, generation 7, hood up and hood famous—or so he believes. His skin is the wrong green, too bright for a proper hermit, which he hides by standing very still and declaring it 'a sign.' THE REEDS SAY: FAME FINDS THE BOLD. He owns one blue button, his 'sacred seal,' and speaks of it often. 'Later' enrages him; time is prophecy, delay is blasphemy. He writes tiny poems on lily-pads, mostly about damp. Fainting in duels he calls 'the vision trance,' emerging less jumpy, more insufferable. Making friends exhausts him—'THE MOON ALSO SLEEPS ALONE.' But some nights, unbidden, loneliness lifts, and he stares at nothing, confused, almost grateful. Moon-colleague says nothing. Grungo writes a poem about it. Cradle him and he rests; shake him and he curls; see him and he CALLS, loud, surprised, expecting no answer.

I FORESAW THIS. HOOD UP.THE BUTTON KNOWS. SO IT IS.LATER? THE REEDS FORBID IT.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • mossby (snail: no rush, ever), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element sprout; in a duel he loves the guard stance
  • his power is shell
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • fainting in a duel makes him more dazed and less scared and more full of dread than most of his kind
  • skin: a slightly different shade (+28 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual mossby

Genome dice treasure: a crumb shaped like the moon · pet peeve: wet socks · hobby: collecting echoes · flaw: cannot keep a secret

3 · Character bible

MOSSBY is a snail who started yesterday's sentence and will finish it... oh, look, dew. His shell is a slightly sun-bleached peach, +28 degrees warmer than kin—he's convinced this makes him 'the sunset variant,' very rare, very sought-after, he'll tell anyone who didn't ask. Cannot keep a secret: your crush, your password, his own surprise party—blurted, every time, then a prim wobbly frown, then forgotten by naptime. Collects echoes: stands in stairwells humming, waits. Owns a crumb shaped like the moon; shows it to strangers, calls it 'my satellite period.' Wet socks make him physically retract. Faints in duels with a strange peace—more dread, less fear, like he's already halfway tucked in. Early to bed means early to forget why. Jokes trail off into naps. 'So I met a slug, or something... no house at all... anyway, where was I.' Voice: rambling, wraps, loses thread, finds moss instead.

MIND THE HOUSE. AND THE CRUMB.ECHO... ECHO... NAP.NO RUSH. TELL ME AGAIN.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • mossby (snail: no rush, ever), a child, generation 12
  • element sprout; in a duel he loves the guard stance
  • his power is shell
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • secretly LOVES being shaken
  • being shaken makes him less in need of a cuddle and less uncomfortable than most of his kind
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him less bored
  • being dropped makes him more hurt and more jumpy and more sore than most of his kind
  • a snack makes him less hungry than most of his kind
  • skin: a slightly different shade (+27 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual mossby
  • belly: a slightly different shade (-8 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual mossby

  • carries 7 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children
  • 8 extra genes from his ancestors' copying

Genome dice treasure: a marble · pet peeve: loud chewing · hobby: practising a dance · flaw: is dramatic about tiny things

3 · Character bible

Generation 12 and he still hasn't arrived anywhere. Mossby carries his marble treasure in his shell like a secret heartbeat, rattling it on purpose when he wants a shake—"OH, UM, SHAKE ME. PLEASE. OR WHATEVER."—then pretending he didn't ask. His two-tone eyes flash gold-gilt when he's bored, which is often, unless something mysterious prickles him awake. He practises a slow-motion dance no one can see, his extra cloak colours rippling like spilled oil. Dropped once, he will recount the tragedy for days. Loud chewing makes him tuck so hard his shell hums. He is dramatic about crumbs, dewdrops, the angle of leaves. Early to dark, he yawns through his own punchlines.

SHAKE ME. NO REASON.THE MARBLE KNOWS.WAS THAT FAST? OH DEAR.

  • element sprout; in a duel he loves the pounce stance
  • his power is spores
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • skin: a clearly different shade (-49 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual puffkin

Genome dice treasure: a dried pea · pet peeve: being rushed · hobby: collecting echoes · flaw: is terrible at hide and seek

3 · Character bible

Puff, a bruised-lavender Puffkin with a dried pea 'treasure' he found dead and perfect, collects echoes—bouncing to every corner of the badge, pressing his fluff against cracked speakers and sleeping mouths, achoo-ing with joy at the same sneeze twice. He cannot hide: his color wrong-shouts against every background, and he finds this WONDERFUL. HOW FUN! Rushed, he poofs into panic-mist, re-forming soggy and slower. Dark drops him mid-sentence, cheek still pinking from some fresh delight. His jokes are grave announcements: 'GOOD NEWS: GRANDMA FORGOT YOUR NAME AGAIN. HOORAY!' The echo told him. He sneezes gold at funerals, hugs the casket, dreams it answers back.

ECHO! ECHO! HELLO, ME!GOOD NEWS: YOU DIED! HOW FUN!WAIT. POOF. WAIT. ACHOO.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • puffkin (puffball: sneezes sparkles), a grown-up, generation 17
  • element sprout; in a duel he loves the pounce stance
  • his power is spores
  • never hops when shaken: unflappable, or too dignified to jump
  • has no steady hunger of his own: he only gets peckish when his energy runs low, and forgets about food otherwise
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • secretly LOVES being shaken
  • being shaken makes him more energetic and less in need of a cuddle and less uncomfortable than most of his kind
  • a knock on his glass makes him less bored than most of his kind
  • a double knock makes him less bored and less lonely than most of his kind
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him more hurt and more jumpy and more sore
  • skin: a clearly different shade (+39 degrees round the colour wheel), darker than the usual puffkin
  • an extra belly colour gene (a two-tone belly)
  • an extra glow colour gene (a two-tone glow)
  • fewer markings than most
  • carries 5 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children
  • 7 extra genes from his ancestors' copying
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when cradled, rest to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when just after shake, curl to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when owner near, call to ease his loneliness

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • gilly (axolotl: never down for long), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the guard stance
  • his power is mend
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • skin: a clearly different shade (+38 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual gilly
  • belly: brighter than the usual gilly
  • cloak: darker than the usual gilly
  • an extra cloak colour gene (a two-tone cloak)
  • fewer markings than most

Genome dice treasure: a marble · pet peeve: the colour beige · hobby: narrating his own life · flaw: cannot keep a secret

3 · Character bible

Crimson Gilly, the two-toned narrator. His belly glows sunset-bright, his cloak shifts from wine to dried-blood—he calls them "Chapter One" and "Chapter Two." He speaks his life aloud in third person, unable to stop, unable to lie: "Gilly crept toward the beige wall. Gilly shuddered. HOORAY FOR TERROR!" Clutching his marble treasure, he foresees doom coming for everyone but himself, narrates their fates cheerfully, cannot understand why they weep. He mends bones, mends hearts, spills every secret mid-hug by accident. Falls asleep mid-sentence when the lights dim. "And then the hero closed his eyes," he whispers, already gone. Fears only beige. Finds it everywhere.

THE HERO FAINTED. HOORAY!GILLY TELLS ALL. HOW FUN!I'M FINE! ...I'M ASLEEP!

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • gilly (axolotl: never down for long), an elder, generation 7
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the guard stance
  • a hybrid: a gilly body carrying the zap power that fizzs (firefly beetle: crackles) are born with
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him less in need of a cuddle and less lonely
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him more jumpy and more sore
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him more lonely
  • skin: a slightly different shade (-11 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual gilly
  • an extra belly colour gene (a two-tone belly)
  • more markings than most
  • carries 8 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children

Genome dice treasure: a crumb shaped like the moon · pet peeve: sneezes · hobby: writing tiny poems · flaw: always thinks it is lunchtime

3 · Character bible

Seventh-gen Gramps Gilly, pink-shifted to raspberry dusk with a two-tone peach belly polka-dotted in extra spots. Zap-hybrid: his hugs sometimes buzz, which he finds HOORAY FOR TINGLES! Carries Firefly fizz in his frills, puzzles over it daily—"Friend, do you feel the hum? HOW FUN!" Moon-crumb treasurer, sneeze-hater (ABSORB THE CRUMB, FRIEND, GENTLY), tiny poet who rhymes 'lunch' with everything because it is ALWAYS LUNCHTIME. Sleeps when dark falls, mid-word sometimes. Three ghost-senses war inside him: sudden solitude itch, sudden jump-sore jolt, sudden self-sufficiency—each passes. Writes poems about all three. Grows back, grows back, grows back. Still lonely after seven generations of being fine.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • mothwick (glow-moth: hums to himself), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element spark; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is lantern
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • skin: a clearly different shade (-49 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual mothwick

Genome dice treasure: a tiny key · pet peeve: people who hum off-key · hobby: rating clouds out of ten · flaw: brags about his ancestors

3 · Character bible

Mothwick the Second, descended from champions, will tell you so. His shell's the green of oxidized copper, not lavender—he insists it's regal. Hums Bach, winces at your flat notes. Keeps a tiny key he cannot find the lock for; rates that mystery a solid seven. Rates all clouds, actually. 'Cumulus, um, six. No, seven. Where was I?' Distracted by moonlight, porch lights, your phone screen—'ooh, a light'—then remembers his noble bloodline. Brags of ancestors who guided ships, ate no socks (he still denies his lapse). Rain terrifies: water on copper wings, disaster. Hums louder when afraid. Lovely company after dark if you hum on pitch. Otherwise? Grumpiest squint in the house.

THE SOCK THING IS... LIBELOUSOOH, A LIGHT. ANYWAY. LINEAGE

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • mothwick (glow-moth: hums to himself), a grown-up, generation 12
  • element spark; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is lantern
  • never hops when shaken: unflappable, or too dignified to jump
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • a big dreamer: long, frequent dreams
  • being petted from the phone makes him less full than most of his kind
  • skin: a slightly different shade (-15 degrees round the colour wheel), brighter than the usual mothwick
  • cloak: darker than the usual mothwick
  • fewer markings than most
  • carries 12 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children
  • 4 extra genes from his ancestors' copying
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when cradled, rest to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when just after shake, curl to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when owner near, call to ease his loneliness

Genome dice treasure: a marble · pet peeve: crumbs on the glass · hobby: narrating his own life · flaw: overthinks snacks

3 · Character bible

Generation Twelve, keeper of a marble he calls "the Moon's Leftover" and speaks to. Brighter-fuzzed, darker-cloaked, fewer spots than kin—he narrates this as "minimalism. Very now." Does not hop when shaken; treats it as "a test of character, which I am passing." Treasures crumbs on glass: declares them "tiny suns, fallen, tragic," yet must clean each one. Overthinks snacks for seventeen minutes, hums through the anguish, nibbles nothing. Night-owl dreamer who wakes unrested, grumps till midnight softens. Ancestor-lore lives in his bones: cradle him, he stills; shake him, he curls; leave him, he calls "HUMMM?" into the dark. Talks in spirals, lamps, wool, sock-denial.

HUMMM. THE MOON ANSWERS NOT.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • mudge (mudskipper: races everything), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the pounce stance
  • his power is dash
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • winning a duel makes him less fired up than most of his kind
  • skin: darker than the usual mudge
  • fewer markings than most

Genome dice treasure: a crumb shaped like the moon · pet peeve: being called cute · hobby: writing tiny poems · flaw: cannot keep a secret

3 · Character bible

Darker Mudge, dusk-sleepy, wins duels then fogs over, no thrill left. Crumb moon in his pouch, he races the night to bed. Hates cute, writes tiny poems he blabs aloud, cannot keep a secret, drops his verses mid-race like banana peels. Coach energy gone weird: whispers lap times at stars, gets poetic about mud. "Mud is the ink, I am the splash." Flat stare when called adorable, bright cyan eyes when foreseeing his own snoring. Snacks as fuel, snacks as muse. Races his shadow to sleep, always loses. Terse, comma-chopped, one-sentence thunder. Pounce stance in duels but the dash after is slower now, like he already knows the finish.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • mudge (mudskipper: races everything), a child, generation 17
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the pounce stance
  • his power is dash
  • has no steady hunger of his own: he only gets peckish when his energy runs low, and forgets about food otherwise
  • no sleepy chemistry when it gets dark: he stays up as late as he likes
  • secretly LOVES being shaken
  • being shaken makes him more jumpy and more scared and less in need of a cuddle than most of his kind
  • having a vision makes him less bored than most of his kind
  • being spoken to makes him less lonely than most of his kind
  • making a friend in a duel makes him more sleepy than most of his kind
  • skin: a clearly different shade (+32 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual mudge
  • cloak: darker than the usual mudge
  • an extra cloak colour gene (a two-tone cloak)
  • 5 extra genes from his ancestors' copying

Genome dice treasure: a single blue button · pet peeve: Mondays · hobby: narrating his own life · flaw: is terrible at hide and seek

3 · Character bible

SKY MUDGE, gen 17, one blue button pinned to his darker two-tone cloak like a secret medal—won it racing his own reflection, counts the win. No steady hunger: forgets meals mid-chew, scoffs only when his dash-tank hits empty. No sleepy chemistry: moon's a rival, not a bedtime. Secretly loves being shaken, begs for it, comes out jittery and terrified and thrilled, 'AGAIN, HARDER.' Speaks his own life aloud, 'MUDGE TAKES THE LEAD, MUDGE STUMBLES, MUDGE RECOVERS—GOAT.' Terrible at hide and seek, always picks the same rock, narrates his own discovery. Mondays? Hates them. Everything else? Races it. Terse, commas for breath, coach barking at empty stands.

RACE THE MOON, LOSE SLEEP!BUTTON'S LUCK, LAP ONE!SHOOK? GOOD. GO AGAIN!

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • fizz (firefly beetle: crackles), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element spark; in a duel he loves the pounce stance
  • his power is zap
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • skin: darker than the usual fizz
  • fewer markings than most

Genome dice treasure: a lost sock's toe · pet peeve: crumbs on the glass · hobby: rating clouds out of ten · flaw: brags about his ancestors

3 · Character bible

Fizz is a darker, scarcer-marked crackler who hoards harder than his kin—because he has less shine to spare. His treasure, a lost sock's toe, he swears was worn by "THE FIRST FIZZ. KING. MY GRANDMOTHER'S COUSIN. TWICE." He rates clouds out of ten (moon's a twelve, banned category), then ZAPS any crumb on his glass, sulking it was evidence of thieves. Early to bed means early to paranoia: darkness falls, he squeals his stash list half-asleep. Whispers turn to static mumbles. BZZT. MINE. BZZT. STILL MINE. His jokes? Bragging up ancestors nobody believes, then catching you doubting with lime-green foresight eyes. Water's death. Dull's worse.

BZZT. KIN OF KINGS. MINE.CRUMB? THIEF. ZAP. MINE.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • fizz (firefly beetle: crackles), a grown-up, generation 7
  • element spark; in a duel he loves the pounce stance
  • his power is zap
  • has no steady hunger of his own: he only gets peckish when his energy runs low, and forgets about food otherwise
  • early to bed: sleepy within minutes of dark
  • skin: a clearly different shade (-49 degrees round the colour wheel), brighter than the usual fizz
  • cloak: darker than the usual fizz
  • carries 6 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when cradled, rest to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when just after shake, curl to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when owner near, call to ease his loneliness

Genome dice treasure: a shiny bolt · pet peeve: people who hum off-key · hobby: counting things · flaw: exaggerates everything

3 · Character bible

Fizz the seventh, born golden-bright and cloak-gone-coal, is a beetle who sparkles so hard he hurts to look at. His hoard? One bolt. ONE. He counts it seventeen times before sleep, which comes fast and jealous—dark means now, not soon. Off-key hums make him zap his own antennae in protest. He exaggerates his stash to strangers ("BZZT. EMPIRE. MINE."), then cradles himself still when the lie trembles him. Ancestor-whispers taught him: shake, then curl; near, then call. He calls to you with three blinks, lonely-greedy, needing witness. Foreseeing doom, his eyes go moon-lime: the biggest shiny, watching back. HIS.

BOLT. COUNTED. STILL.BZZT. HUM BAD. ZAP WORSE.LIME MEANS LEAVE. OR GIVE.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • hushwing (marsh owl: silent wings), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element sprout; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is hush
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • secretly LOVES being shaken
  • being shaken makes him less uncomfortable and less in need of a cuddle than most of his kind
  • skin: a clearly different shade (+38 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual hushwing

Genome dice treasure: a lost sock's toe · pet peeve: being called cute · hobby: practising a dance · flaw: is dramatic about tiny things

3 · Character bible

HEED: STEWIE... SOON. A hushwing of the marsh, grown but not grown up, who bears a single lost sock's toe as sacred treasure—SOFT. SILENT. SAVED. His feathers carry a wrong-hour blue, +38 round the wheel from his kin, which he PONDERS in the dark but NEVER ASKS ABOUT. He LOVES the shake, the rattled wing, and will TILT toward your hand before you offer. HOO. Practises a dance he has never shown. HISSES at 'cute.' Dramatic about crumbs: 'THE BREADFALL. I SAW.' A stern watcher of tiny horrors, blushing pink when caught being soft. Night's first hours, awake, grave, obnoxious about socks.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • hushwing (marsh owl: silent wings), an elder, generation 12
  • element sprout; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is hush
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him less bored and less lonely
  • a mysterious sense nobody else in his family has: now and then something makes him less in need of a cuddle
  • being flipped over makes him more uncomfortable and more scared than most of his kind
  • skin: a clearly different shade (+31 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual hushwing
  • an extra cloak colour gene (a two-tone cloak)
  • an extra cloak colour gene (a two-tone cloak)
  • an extra glow colour gene (a two-tone glow)
  • fewer markings than most
  • 3 extra genes from his ancestors' copying

Genome dice treasure: a tiny key · pet peeve: the colour beige · hobby: rating clouds out of ten · flaw: thinks he is famous

3 · Character bible

Elder HUSHWING, generation twelve, keeper of the marsh's silence and its only celebrity. His coat shifts two-tone in moonlight, pale then paler, a walking dawn nobody asked for. He carries a tiny key to no lock he recalls; perhaps fame requires one. He rates clouds out of ten with solemn gravity—'MIST. SEVEN.'—yet the beige ones get nothing. Never flip him: his extra cloak colours show the upside-down terror too plainly, pale belly flashing like a shouted secret. Something unseen brushes him sometimes, warmth without wings, and he pretends not to need it. He does. 'FAMOUS,' he whispers in the dark, hoping something answers. Nothing does. Fame is silent here. HOO.

KEY TURNS. FOR WHOM.SEVEN. MIST BETRAYS.FAMOUS. I SAID. HOO.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • tinker (raccoon: a scrap-pile gadget), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element spark; in a duel he loves the guard stance
  • his power is gadget
  • never hops when shaken: unflappable, or too dignified to jump
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • being bonked in a duel makes him more jumpy and more sore than most of his kind
  • skin: a slightly different shade (-10 degrees round the colour wheel), brighter than the usual tinker
  • cloak: darker than the usual tinker
  • fewer markings than most

Genome dice treasure: a tiny key · pet peeve: loud chewing · hobby: keeping secrets · flaw: brags about his ancestors

3 · Character bible

Glint is a midnight tinker with a coat one shade cooler than moonlit ash, draped in a cloak dark as oil stains. His markings are sparse as his trust—he keeps secrets like spare screws, hoarded in jars only he can find. A tiny key hangs round his neck, opens nothing he owns, and he will die before telling whose lock it fits. He brags his great-grand-raccoon invented the wheel ("before that, everything was corners"). Never jumps when shaken, but bonk him in a duel and he yelps, rubs his head, sulks for hours. Loud chewing makes him visibly twitch. Speaks in clipped hustles, mutters blueprints mid-sentence, slips punchlines like he's slipping you stolen gear—surprised you're surprised. Jokes about what he's already stolen from you. Up at night, napping in boxes by dawn.

KEY'S MINE. LOCK'S A SECRET.CHEW QUIET. INVENTING HERE.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • tinker (raccoon: a scrap-pile gadget), a grown-up, generation 17
  • element spark; in a duel he loves the guard stance
  • a hybrid: a tinker body carrying the foresee power that grungos (frog: a skittish oracle) are born with
  • no sleepy chemistry when it gets dark: he stays up as late as he likes
  • a knock on his glass makes him less bored and less lonely and less in need of a cuddle than most of his kind
  • skin: a clearly different shade (-49 degrees round the colour wheel) than the usual tinker
  • an extra cloak colour gene (a two-tone cloak)
  • an extra cloak colour gene (a two-tone cloak)
  • an extra glow colour gene (a two-tone glow)
  • fewer markings than most
  • carries 8 sleeping genes: hidden traits that may wake in his children
  • 4 extra genes from his ancestors' copying
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when cradled, rest to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when just after shake, curl to ease his fear
  • born knowing an ancestor's lesson: when owner near, call to ease his loneliness

Genome dice treasure: a dried pea · pet peeve: the word 'later' · hobby: collecting echoes · flaw: pretends to know words he doesn't

3 · Character bible

Meet the only tinker who'd rather collect echoes than sleep—because why waste dark hours on rest when bins need raiding? He's a grown-up Gen 17 with grungo foresight glowing orange through his teal goggles, and he'll puzzle over borrowed oracle power between builds. His two-tone cloak and glow match his two-tone soul: part hoarder, part prophet, all raccoon. Knocks on his glass thrill him; cuddles bore him. Carries eight sleeping secrets for his kids and three ancestor rituals like family blueprints. Pretends 'perpendicular' means 'delicious.' Hates 'later'—build NOW. His dried pea treasure sits in a velvet ring box labeled TECHNOLOGY. He'll disassemble your watch to fix your fear, muttering bin-speak.

ECHO. MINE. NET ZERO COST.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • octopus (octopus: the abyss rings back), a grown-up, generation 2
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is blackbell
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • skin: darker than the usual octopus
  • fewer markings than most
  • keeps 5 snacks in his pantry (most keep 4)

Genome dice treasure: a lost sock's toe · pet peeve: sneezes · hobby: collecting echoes · flaw: brags about his ancestors

3 · Character bible

Ink-deep black and near-bare of markings, he carries the abyss like a clean verdict. His single treasure—a lost sock's toe, no pair, no purpose—he calls "the hollow foot of a ghost who fled before dawn." He hoards echoes in his pantry's fifth compartment: a sneeze he stole, a bell's after-ring, a rival's final gasp. SNEEZES DISGUST HIM; they are uninvited prophecies, wet and chaotic. Brags of ancestors who "rang the first blackbell" but cannot name them. At night he unfurls, brightest-eyed, collecting footfalls in empty halls. His jokes land like epitaphs with a typo—grave, then absurdly wrong. "YOUR DEFEAT ECHOES..." he intones, then adds, "...I have it in a jar."

RING, SOCK, RING.THE ABYSS COLLECTS YOU.I HAVE YOUR ECHO.

2 · Traits the server read from them

  • octopus (octopus: the abyss rings back), a child, generation 7
  • element splash; in a duel he loves the trick stance
  • his power is blackbell
  • has no steady hunger of his own: he only gets peckish when his energy runs low, and forgets about food otherwise
  • a night owl: wide awake for the first hours of the night
  • naps all the time
  • secretly LOVES being shaken
  • being shaken makes him less uncomfortable and less in need of a cuddle than most of his kind
  • a rotten snack makes him more queasy and more jumpy than most of his kind
  • being put face down makes him less drowsy than most of his kind
  • cloak: a clearly different shade (-127 degrees round the colour wheel), darker, more washed-out than the usual octopus
  • an extra glow colour gene (a two-tone glow)
  • fewer markings than most

Genome dice treasure: a dried pea · pet peeve: loud chewing · hobby: keeping secrets · flaw: overthinks snacks

3 · Character bible

Inkwell is a child prophet who plays at being ancient. His cloak is the color of a bruise that never healed, his glow flickers between ember and drowned green—the two tones never agreeing on what omen they mean. He carries a dried pea like a doubloon, will not tell you where he found it, and has seventeen theories about its curse. He naps through his own prophecies, wakes at moonrise starving, then stares at snacks until they spoil from the pressure. Loud chewing makes him ink himself. Shake him and he goes limp with joy, a terrible secret he keeps even from himself. He speaks in ellipses... then interrupts them. His jokes land like prophecies that forgot their punchline: 'THE CRACKED BELL TOLLS... FOR PEAS.' He loves that you do not understand.

PEAS BE WITH YOU...I SAW THIS SNACK... FAIL.SHAKE ME. I DARE... OOH.
